# Skills · G · part 101

920 entries, grill to grill-me. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **grill**: Relentless questioning of an idea before it becomes a milestone, through the vendored superpowers brainstorming procedure with ytstack's pitch, project one-liner and decisions injected; writes the agreed design into the milestone context. Use between office-hours and plan-milestone, when a feature request is bigger than one sentence, or when the user says grill me, poke holes in this, let's think this through, design before we plan. Not for one-sentence changes (quick-task) and not for slicing or task plans (slice-milestone, plan-task). ([Yesterday-AI/ytstack](https://github.com/Yesterday-AI/ytstack/tree/HEAD/skills/grill) · ★2)
- **grill**: 用于计划或设计审查——需求模糊、需要权衡、设计方案需要质疑、或需要明确设计决策时使用。把设计建模为决策树并按轮次提问，用户回答后重新整理下一轮可以提问的决策，直到所有分支穷尽、达成共识。只要涉及设计和方案相关的问题，就请加载此技能。 ([418-teapot/ZooKeeper](https://github.com/418-teapot/ZooKeeper/tree/HEAD/skills/grill) · ★1)

- **grill**: Before creating anything, get grilled about it. Interview yourself relentlessly about every decision until shared understanding is reached. Prevents the #1 failure: building the wrong thing. Use when about to create a skill, start a feature, design a system, or plan any non-trivial work. Also use when user says grill me, question my plan, challenge this, stress-test, or validate my thinking. ([Adit-Jain-srm/Razorpay-AI-Builder](https://github.com/Adit-Jain-srm/Razorpay-AI-Builder/tree/HEAD/.cursor/skills/skill-forge/skills/grill) · ★1 · 2 copies)
